   ```powershell
   cd "$env:USERPROFILE\Desktop\html\next\fishing-website"
   ```

2. **Install dependencies** (if they aren’t already installed):

   ```powershell

   ```

3. **Run the development server**:

   ```powershell
   npm run dev   # or `yarn dev`
   ```

4. Open your browser and go to **http://localhost:3000** – you’ll see the default Next.js welcome page.
